  var nextSlide = function nextSlide() {
    return setCurrentSlide(function (prev) {
      return (prev + 1) % GALLERY_SLIDES.length;
    });
  };
  var prevSlide = function prevSlide() {
    return setCurrentSlide(function (prev) {
      return (prev - 1 + GALLERY_SLIDES.length) % GALLERY_SLIDES.length;
    });
  };
  var scrollToSection = function scrollToSection(id) {
    setActiveNav(id);
    if (currentView !== 'site') setCurrentView('site');
    var element = document.getElementById(id);
    if (element) element.scrollIntoView({
      behavior: 'smooth'
    });
  };
  var switchRole = function switchRole(roleKey) {
    if (roleKey === 'dono') {
      setCurrentUser({
        name: 'Luis Valverde',
        email: 'luis@valverds.com',
        role: 'dono',
        barberKey: 'luis',
        title: 'Propriet├írio & Mestre Barbeiro',
        avatar: 'luis_valverde.jpg'
      });
      setCurrentView('reception');
      setReceptionTab('agenda');
    } else if (roleKey === 'barbeiro') {
      setCurrentUser({
        name: 'Yago Barber',
        email: 'yago@valverds.com',
        role: 'barbeiro',
        barberKey: 'yago',
        title: 'Especialista em Fade & Estilo',
        avatar: 'yago_barber.png'
      });
      var yagoSched = barberSchedules.find(function (b) {
        return b.id === 'yago';
      }) || INITIAL_BARBER_SCHEDULES[1];
      setBarberEditStartTime(yagoSched.startTime);
      setBarberEditEndTime(yagoSched.endTime);
      setBarberEditLunchBreak(yagoSched.lunchBreak);
      setBarberEditActiveDays(yagoSched.activeDays);
      setCurrentView('reception');
      setBarberTab('minha_agenda');
    } else if (roleKey === 'recepcao') {
      setCurrentUser({
        name: 'Recep├º├úo Valverd\'s',
        email: 'recepcao@valverds.com',
        role: 'recepcao',
        barberKey: 'recepcao',
        title: 'Gerente de Recep├º├úo & Caixa',
        avatar: 'logo_valverds_clean.png'
      });
      setCurrentView('reception');
      setReceptionTab('agenda');
    } else if (roleKey === 'cliente') {
      setCurrentUser({
        name: clientProfile.name,
        email: clientProfile.email,
        role: 'cliente',
        barberKey: 'cliente',
        title: clientProfile.tier,
        avatar: 'logo_valverds_clean.png'
      });
      setCurrentView('client');
      setClientTab('agendamentos');
    }
  };
  var todaysAppointments = useMemo(function () {
    return appointments.filter(function (a) {
      return a.day === 'Seg' && (a.weekId === 0 || a.weekId === undefined);
    });
  }, [appointments]);
  var completedToday = todaysAppointments.filter(function (a) {
    return a.status === 'concluido';
  });
  var inChairToday = todaysAppointments.filter(function (a) {
    return a.status === 'em_atendimento';
  });
  var waitingToday = todaysAppointments.filter(function (a) {
    return a.status === 'aguardando';
  });
  var scheduledToday = todaysAppointments.filter(function (a) {
    return a.status === 'agendado';
  });
  var revenueServices = completedToday.reduce(function (acc, a) {
    return acc + (a.total || a.price);
  }, 0);
  var revenueBalcao = counterSales.reduce(function (acc, s) {
    return acc + s.total;
  }, 0);
  var totalRevenueToday = revenueServices + revenueBalcao;
  var currentBarberName = currentUser.role === 'barbeiro' ? currentUser.name : currentUser.role === 'dono' ? 'Luis Valverde' : '';
  var myAllAppointments = useMemo(function () {
    return appointments.filter(function (a) {
      return a.barber === currentBarberName;
    });
  }, [appointments, currentBarberName]);
  var myWeekAppointments = useMemo(function () {
    return myAllAppointments.filter(function (a) {
      return a.weekId === currentWeek.id || currentWeek.id === 0 && a.weekId === undefined;
    });
  }, [myAllAppointments, currentWeek]);
  var myTodaysAppointments = useMemo(function () {
    return myAllAppointments.filter(function (a) {
      return a.day === 'Seg' && (a.weekId === 0 || a.weekId === undefined);
    });
  }, [myAllAppointments]);
  var myCompletedToday = myTodaysAppointments.filter(function (a) {
    return a.status === 'concluido';
  });
  var myRevenueToday = myCompletedToday.reduce(function (acc, a) {
    return acc + a.price;
  }, 0);
  var myCommissionRate = currentUser.name.includes('Luis') ? 0.60 : 0.50;
  var myCommissionToday = myRevenueToday * myCommissionRate;
  var updateStock = function updateStock(id, delta) {
    setProducts(function (prev) {
      return prev.map(function (item) {
        return item.id === id ? _objectSpread(_objectSpread({}, item), {}, {
          stock: Math.max(0, item.stock + delta)
        }) : item;
      });
    });
  };
  var changeAppointmentStatus = function changeAppointmentStatus(id, newStatus) {
    setAppointments(function (prev) {
      return prev.map(function (a) {
        return a.id === id ? _objectSpread(_objectSpread({}, a), {}, {
          status: newStatus
        }) : a;
      });
    });
  };
  var deleteAppointment = function deleteAppointment(id) {
    if (confirm('Deseja realmente cancelar e remover este agendamento?')) {
      setAppointments(function (prev) {
        return prev.filter(function (a) {
          return a.id !== id;
        });
      });
    }
  };
  var openEditAppointment = function openEditAppointment(appt) {
    setEditingAppt(_objectSpread({}, appt));
    setIsEditApptModalOpen(true);
  };
  var handleSaveEditedAppt = function handleSaveEditedAppt(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setAppointments(function (prev) {
      return prev.map(function (a) {
        return a.id === editingAppt.id ? editingAppt : a;
      });
    });
    setIsEditApptModalOpen(false);
  };
  var handleCreateBooking = function handleCreateBooking(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!bookingForm.client || !bookingForm.phone) {
      alert('Por favor preencha nome e WhatsApp.');
      return;
    }
    var srv = services.find(function (s) {
      return s.id === bookingForm.serviceId;
    }) || services[0];
    var brb = barbers.find(function (b) {
      return b.barberKey === bookingForm.barberKey;
    }) || barbers[0];
    var newAppt = {
      id: Date.now(),
      weekId: currentWeek.id,
      day: bookingForm.day,
      date: bookingForm.day === 'Seg' ? '08/09' : bookingForm.day === 'Ter' ? '09/09' : '10/09',
      dayNum: bookingForm.day === 'Seg' ? 8 : bookingForm.day === 'Ter' ? 9 : 10,
      client: bookingForm.client,
      phone: bookingForm.phone,
      service: srv.name,
      barber: brb.name,
      time: bookingForm.time,
      status: 'agendado',
      price: srv.price,
      items: [{
        name: srv.name,
        price: srv.price
      }],
      total: srv.price,
      paymentMethod: '',
      notes: bookingForm.notes || 'Agendado pelo painel.'
    };
    setAppointments(function (prev) {
      return [newAppt].concat(_toConsumableArray(prev));
    });
    setIsBookingModalOpen(false);
    setBookingForm({
      client: '',
      phone: '',
      serviceId: 's1',
      barberKey: 'luis',
      day: 'Seg',
      time: '14:00',
      notes: ''
    });
  };
  var openCheckout = function openCheckout(appt) {
    setCheckoutAppt(_objectSpread(_objectSpread({}, appt), {}, {
      items: _toConsumableArray(appt.items)
    }));
    setIsCheckoutModalOpen(true);
  };
  var addItemToCheckout = function addItemToCheckout(product) {
    if (!checkoutAppt || !product) return;
    if (product.price > 0 && product.stock <= 0) {
      alert('Item sem estoque dispon├¡vel!');
      return;
    }
    updateStock(product.id, -1);
    setCheckoutAppt(function (prev) {
      return _objectSpread(_objectSpread({}, prev), {}, {
        items: [].concat(_toConsumableArray(prev.items), [{
          name: product.name,
          price: product.price
        }]),
        total: prev.total + product.price
      });
    });
  };
  var handleConfirmCheckout = function handleConfirmCheckout() {
    if (!checkoutAppt) return;
    setAppointments(function (prev) {
      return prev.map(function (a) {
        return a.id === checkoutAppt.id ? _objectSpread(_objectSpread({}, a), {}, {
          status: 'concluido',
          items: checkoutAppt.items,
          total: checkoutAppt.total,
          paymentMethod: selectedPayment
        }) : a;
      });
    });
    setIsCheckoutModalOpen(false);
    setCheckoutAppt(null);
  };
  var handleQuickSale = function handleQuickSale(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!quickSaleProduct) return;
    var total = quickSaleProduct.price * quickSaleQty;
    updateStock(quickSaleProduct.id, -quickSaleQty);
    setCounterSales(function (prev) {
      return [{
        id: Date.now(),
        item: quickSaleProduct.name,
        category: quickSaleProduct.category,
        qty: quickSaleQty,
        total: total,
        time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString('pt-BR', {
          hour: '2-digit',
          minute: '2-digit'
        }),
        paymentMethod: quickSalePayment
      }].concat(_toConsumableArray(prev));
    });
    setIsQuickSaleModalOpen(false);
  };
  var handleAddNewProduct = function handleAddNewProduct(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    var newP = {
      id: 'p' + Date.now(),
      name: newProductForm.name,
      category: newProductForm.category,
      price: parseFloat(newProductForm.price),
      stock: parseInt(newProductForm.stock),
      minStock: parseInt(newProductForm.minStock),
      type: newProductForm.type
    };
    setProducts(function (prev) {
      return [].concat(_toConsumableArray(prev), [newP]);
    });
    setIsNewProductModalOpen(false);
    setNewProductForm({
      name: '',
      category: 'Bar',
      price: 15,
      stock: 24,
      minStock: 8,
      type: 'Cerveja'
    });
  };
  var handleSaveBarberSchedule = function handleSaveBarberSchedule(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    var currentBarberId = currentUser.barberKey || 'yago';
    setBarberSchedules(function (prev) {
      return prev.map(function (b) {
        return b.id === currentBarberId ? _objectSpread(_objectSpread({}, b), {}, {
          startTime: barberEditStartTime,
          endTime: barberEditEndTime,
          lunchBreak: barberEditLunchBreak,
          activeDays: barberEditActiveDays,
          workDaysDesc: "".concat(barberEditActiveDays.join(', '), " (").concat(barberEditStartTime, " \xE0s ").concat(barberEditEndTime, ")")
        }) : b;
      });
    });
    setScheduleSavedToast(true);
    setTimeout(function () {
      return setScheduleSavedToast(false);
    }, 4000);
  };
  var toggleBarberDay = function toggleBarberDay(day) {
    if (barberEditActiveDays.includes(day)) {
      setBarberEditActiveDays(barberEditActiveDays.filter(function (d) {
        return d !== day;
      }));
    } else {
      setBarberEditActiveDays([].concat(_toConsumableArray(barberEditActiveDays), [day]));
    }
  };

    // =========================================================================
    // COMPONENTE PRINCIPAL
    // =========================================================================
    function ValverdsApp() {
      const [currentView, setCurrentView] = useState('site'); // 'site' | 'reception' | 'client'
      const [activeNav, setActiveNav] = useState('home');

      const [currentUser, setCurrentUser] = useState({
        name: 'Luis Valverde',
        email: 'luis@valverds.com',
        role: 'dono',
        barberKey: 'luis',
        title: 'Propriet├írio & Mestre Barbeiro',
        avatar: 'luis_valverde.jpg'
      });

      const [receptionTab, setReceptionTab] = useState('agenda');
      const [barberTab, setBarberTab] = useState('minha_agenda');
      const [barberCalendarMode, setBarberCalendarMode] = useState('semanal');
      const [selectedWeekIndex, setSelectedWeekIndex] = useState(1);
      const [selectedWeekDay, setSelectedWeekDay] = useState('Seg');
      const [selectedMonthDay, setSelectedMonthDay] = useState(8);

      const [clientTab, setClientTab] = useState('agendamentos');

      const [appointments, setAppointments] = useState(INITIAL_APPOINTMENTS);
      const [products, setProducts] = useState(INITIAL_PRODUCTS);
      const [services, setServices] = useState(INITIAL_SERVICES);
      const [barbers, setBarbers] = useState(INITIAL_BARBERS);
      const [shopHours, setShopHours] = useState(INITIAL_SHOP_HOURS);
      const [barberSchedules, setBarberSchedules] = useState(INITIAL_BARBER_SCHEDULES);
      const [clientProfile, setClientProfile] = useState(INITIAL_CLIENT_PROFILE);

      const [barberEditStartTime, setBarberEditStartTime] = useState('10:00');
      const [barberEditEndTime, setBarberEditEndTime] = useState('19:00');
      const [barberEditLunchBreak, setBarberEditLunchBreak] = useState('13:00 ├ás 14:00');
      const [barberEditActiveDays, setBarberEditActiveDays] = useState(['Ter', 'Qua', 'Qui', 'Sex', 'S├íb']);
      const [scheduleSavedToast, setScheduleSavedToast] = useState(false);

      const [isBookingModalOpen, setIsBookingModalOpen] = useState(false);
      const [isEditApptModalOpen, setIsEditApptModalOpen] = useState(false);
      const [editingAppt, setEditingAppt] = useState(null);

      const [isCheckoutModalOpen, setIsCheckoutModalOpen] = useState(false);
      const [checkoutAppt, setCheckoutAppt] = useState(null);
      const [selectedPayment, setSelectedPayment] = useState('PIX');
      const [additionalItemId, setAdditionalItemId] = useState('');

      const [isQuickSaleModalOpen, setIsQuickSaleModalOpen] = useState(false);
      const [quickSaleProduct, setQuickSaleProduct] = useState(INITIAL_PRODUCTS[0]);
      const [quickSaleQty, setQuickSaleQty] = useState(1);
      const [quickSalePayment, setQuickSalePayment] = useState('PIX');

      const [isNewProductModalOpen, setIsNewProductModalOpen] = useState(false);
      const [newProductForm, setNewProductForm] = useState({ name: '', category: 'Bar', price: 15, stock: 24, minStock: 8, type: 'Cerveja' });

      const [bookingForm, setBookingForm] = useState({
        client: '',
        phone: '',
        serviceId: 's1',
        barberKey: 'luis',
        day: 'Seg',
        time: '14:00',
        notes: ''
      });

      const [counterSales, setCounterSales] = useState([
        { id: 901, item: 'Pomada Modeladora Matte Valverd\'s (150g)', category: 'Produtos', qty: 1, total: 45, time: '10:15', paymentMethod: 'PIX' },
        { id: 902, item: 'Cerveja IPA Artesanal Valverd\'s (500ml)', category: 'Bar', qty: 2, total: 40, time: '11:20', paymentMethod: 'CART├âO' }
      ]);

      const [currentSlide, setCurrentSlide] = useState(0);
      const currentWeek = WEEKS_DATA[selectedWeekIndex] || WEEKS_DATA[1];
      const nextSlide = () => setCurrentSlide((prev) => (prev + 1) % GALLERY_SLIDES.length);
      const prevSlide = () => setCurrentSlide((prev) => (prev - 1 + GALLERY_SLIDES.length) % GALLERY_SLIDES.length);

      const scrollToSection = (id) => {
        setActiveNav(id);
        if (currentView !== 'site') setCurrentView('site');
        const element = document.getElementById(id);
        if (element) element.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
      };

      const switchRole = (roleKey) => {
        if (roleKey === 'dono') {
          setCurrentUser({
            name: 'Luis Valverde',
            email: 'luis@valverds.com',
            role: 'dono',
            barberKey: 'luis',
            title: 'Propriet├írio & Mestre Barbeiro',
            avatar: 'luis_valverde.jpg'
          });
          setCurrentView('reception');
          setReceptionTab('agenda');
        } else if (roleKey === 'barbeiro') {
          setCurrentUser({
            name: 'Yago Barber',
            email: 'yago@valverds.com',
            role: 'barbeiro',
            barberKey: 'yago',
            title: 'Especialista em Fade & Estilo',
            avatar: 'yago_barber.png'
          });
          const yagoSched = barberSchedules.find(b => b.id === 'yago') || INITIAL_BARBER_SCHEDULES[1];
          setBarberEditStartTime(yagoSched.startTime);
          setBarberEditEndTime(yagoSched.endTime);
          setBarberEditLunchBreak(yagoSched.lunchBreak);
          setBarberEditActiveDays(yagoSched.activeDays);
          setCurrentView('reception');
          setBarberTab('minha_agenda');
        } else if (roleKey === 'recepcao') {
          setCurrentUser({
            name: 'Recep├º├úo Valverd\'s',
            email: 'recepcao@valverds.com',
            role: 'recepcao',
            barberKey: 'recepcao',
            title: 'Gerente de Recep├º├úo & Caixa',
            avatar: 'logo_valverds_clean.png'
          });
          setCurrentView('reception');
          setReceptionTab('agenda');
        } else if (roleKey === 'cliente') {
          setCurrentUser({
            name: clientProfile.name,
            email: clientProfile.email,
            role: 'cliente',
            barberKey: 'cliente',
            title: clientProfile.tier,
            avatar: 'logo_valverds_clean.png'
          });
          setCurrentView('client');
          setClientTab('agendamentos');

      const todaysAppointments = useMemo(() => {
        return appointments.filter(a => a.day === 'Seg' && (a.weekId === 0 || a.weekId === undefined));
      }, [appointments]);

      const completedToday = todaysAppointments.filter(a => a.status === 'concluido');
      const inChairToday = todaysAppointments.filter(a => a.status === 'em_atendimento');
      const waitingToday = todaysAppointments.filter(a => a.status === 'aguardando');
      const scheduledToday = todaysAppointments.filter(a => a.status === 'agendado');

      const revenueServices = completedToday.reduce((acc, a) => acc + (a.total || a.price), 0);
      const revenueBalcao = counterSales.reduce((acc, s) => acc + s.total, 0);
      const totalRevenueToday = revenueServices + revenueBalcao;

      const myAllAppointments = useMemo(() => appointments.filter(a => a.barber === currentBarberName), [appointments, currentBarberName]);
      const myWeekAppointments = useMemo(() => myAllAppointments.filter(a => a.weekId === currentWeek.id || (currentWeek.id === 0 && a.weekId === undefined)), [myAllAppointments, currentWeek]);
      const myTodaysAppointments = useMemo(() => myAllAppointments.filter(a => a.day === 'Seg' && (a.weekId === 0 || a.weekId === undefined)), [myAllAppointments]);
      const myCompletedToday = myTodaysAppointments.filter(a => a.status === 'concluido');
      const myRevenueToday = myCompletedToday.reduce((acc, a) => acc + a.price, 0);
      const myCommissionRate = currentUser.name.includes('Luis') ? 0.60 : 0.50;
      const myCommissionToday = myRevenueToday * myCommissionRate;

      const updateStock = (id, delta) => {
        setProducts(prev => prev.map(item => item.id === id ? { ...item, stock: Math.max(0, item.stock + delta) } : item));
      };

      const changeAppointmentStatus = (id, newStatus) => {
        setAppointments(prev => prev.map(a => a.id === id ? { ...a, status: newStatus } : a));
      };

          setAppointments(prev => prev.filter(a => a.id !== id));

        setEditingAppt({ ...appt });
        setIsEditApptModalOpen(true);
      };

      const handleSaveEditedAppt = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        setAppointments(prev => prev.map(a => a.id === editingAppt.id ? editingAppt : a));
        setIsEditApptModalOpen(false);
      };

      const handleCreateBooking = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!bookingForm.client || !bookingForm.phone) {
          alert('Por favor preencha nome e WhatsApp.');
          return;
        }

        const srv = services.find(s => s.id === bookingForm.serviceId) || services[0];
        const brb = barbers.find(b => b.barberKey === bookingForm.barberKey) || barbers[0];

        const newAppt = {
          id: Date.now(),
          weekId: currentWeek.id,
          day: bookingForm.day,
          date: bookingForm.day === 'Seg' ? '08/09' : bookingForm.day === 'Ter' ? '09/09' : '10/09',
          dayNum: bookingForm.day === 'Seg' ? 8 : bookingForm.day === 'Ter' ? 9 : 10,
          client: bookingForm.client,
          phone: bookingForm.phone,
          service: srv.name,
          barber: brb.name,
          time: bookingForm.time,
          status: 'agendado',
          price: srv.price,
          items: [{ name: srv.name, price: srv.price }],
          total: srv.price,
          paymentMethod: '',
          notes: bookingForm.notes || 'Agendado pelo painel.'
        };

        setAppointments(prev => [newAppt, ...prev]);
        setIsBookingModalOpen(false);
        setBookingForm({ client: '', phone: '', serviceId: 's1', barberKey: 'luis', day: 'Seg', time: '14:00', notes: '' });
      };

      const openCheckout = (appt) => {
        setCheckoutAppt({ ...appt, items: [...appt.items] });
        setIsCheckoutModalOpen(true);
      };

      const addItemToCheckout = (product) => {
        if (!checkoutAppt || !product) return;
        if (product.price > 0 && product.stock <= 0) {
          alert('Item sem estoque dispon├¡vel!');
          return;
        }
        updateStock(product.id, -1);
        setCheckoutAppt(prev => ({
          ...prev,
          items: [...prev.items, { name: product.name, price: product.price }],
          total: prev.total + product.price
        }));
      };

      const handleConfirmCheckout = () => {
        if (!checkoutAppt) return;
        setAppointments(prev => prev.map(a => a.id === checkoutAppt.id ? {
          ...a,
          status: 'concluido',
          items: checkoutAppt.items,
          total: checkoutAppt.total,
          paymentMethod: selectedPayment
        } : a));
        setIsCheckoutModalOpen(false);
        setCheckoutAppt(null);
      };

      const handleQuickSale = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!quickSaleProduct) return;
        const total = quickSaleProduct.price * quickSaleQty;
        updateStock(quickSaleProduct.id, -quickSaleQty);
        setCounterSales(prev => [
          {
            id: Date.now(),
            item: quickSaleProduct.name,
            category: quickSaleProduct.category,
            qty: quickSaleQty,
            total,
            time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString('pt-BR', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
            paymentMethod: quickSalePayment

        ]);
        setIsQuickSaleModalOpen(false);

          category: newProductForm.category,
          price: parseFloat(newProductForm.price),

          {/* ========================================================================= */}

              {/* SIDEBAR VERTICAL ESQUERDA REFINADA */}

'text-[#C9A356] bg-[#161410] border border-[#2A2419]' : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-[#C9A356] hover:bg-[#161410]/50'}`}>

                          <span>{currentUser.role === 'dono' ? 'PROPRIET├üRIO' : currentUser.role === 'barbeiro' ? 'PORTAL DO BARBEIRO' : 'RECEP├ç├âO & BALC├âO'}</span>
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span>{currentUser.name}</span>
                        <span>ÔÇó</span>
                        <span className="text-[#C9A356]">{currentUser.title}</span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <DemoRoleSwitcher />

                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    {currentUser.role === 'barbeiro' ? (
                      <div className="bg-[#181612] px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full border border-[#2D271F] flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 uppercase font-serif">Minha Comiss├úo Hoje:</span>
                        <span className="font-serif font-bold text-sm text-green-400">R$ {myCommissionToday.toFixed(2).replace('.', ',')}</span>
                      </div>
                    ) : (
                      <div className="bg-[#181612] px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full border border-[#2D271F] flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 uppercase font-serif">Faturamento Hoje:</span>
                        <span className="font-serif font-bold text-sm text-green-400">R$ {totalRevenueToday.toFixed(2).replace('.', ',')}</span>
                      </div>
                    )}

                    <button
                      onClick={() => setCurrentView('site')}
                      className="px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#181612] hover:bg-[#25221B] text-gray-300 hover:text-white text-xs font-serif transition-all border border-[#2D271F]"
                    >
                      Site P├║blico
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* CONTROLE DE ABAS (P├ìLULAS) */}
                <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto mt-4 pt-3 border-t border-[#1C1A14] flex items-center gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-1">
                  {currentUser.role === 'barbeiro' ? (
                    <>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => setBarberTab('minha_agenda')}
                        className={`px-5 py-2 rounded-full text-xs font-serif uppercase tracking-wider font-bold transition-all flex items-center gap-2 ${barberTab === 'minha_agenda' ? 'bg-[#C9A356] text-black shadow' : 'bg-[#181612] text-gray-300 hover:bg-[#221F18]'}`}
                      >
                        <Icons.Calendar className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                        <span>Minha Agenda ({barberCalendarMode === 'semanal' ? 'Semana' : barberCalendarMode === 'mensal' ? 'M├¬s' : barberCalendarMode === 'comparativo' ? 'Grade' : 'Hoje'})</span>
                      </button>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => setBarberTab('meus_ganhos')}
                        className={`px-5 py-2 rounded-full text-xs font-serif uppercase tracking-wider font-bold transition-all flex items-center gap-2 ${barberTab === 'meus_ganhos' ? 'bg-[#C9A356] text-black shadow' : 'bg-[#181612] text-gray-300 hover:bg-[#221F18]'}`}
                      >
                        <Icons.CreditCard className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                        <span>Minhas Comiss├Áes</span>
                      </button>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => setBarberTab('minha_escala')}
                        className={`px-5 py-2 rounded-full text-xs font-serif uppercase tracking-wider font-bold transition-all flex items-center gap-2 ${barberTab === 'minha_escala' ? 'bg-[#C9A356] text-black shadow' : 'bg-[#181612] text-gray-300 hover:bg-[#221F18]'}`}
                      >
                        <Icons.Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                        <span>Minha Escala & Hor├írios</span>
                      </button>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => setReceptionTab('agenda')}
                        className={`px-5 py-2 rounded-full text-xs font-serif uppercase tracking-wider font-bold transition-all flex items-center gap-2 ${receptionTab === 'agenda' ? 'bg-[#C9A356] text-black shadow' : 'bg-[#181612] text-gray-300 hover:bg-[#221F18]'}`}
                      >
                        <Icons.Calendar className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                        <span>Agenda Geral</span>
                      </button>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => setReceptionTab('fila')}
                        className={`px-5 py-2 rounded-full text-xs font-serif uppercase tracking-wider font-bold transition-all flex items-center gap-2 ${receptionTab === 'fila' ? 'bg-[#C9A356] text-black shadow' : 'bg-[#181612] text-gray-300 hover:bg-[#221F18]'}`}
                      >
                        <Icons.Coffee className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                        <span>Fila & Lounge</span>
                      </button>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => setReceptionTab('produtos')}
                        className={`px-5 py-2 rounded-full text-xs font-serif uppercase tracking-wider font-bold transition-all flex items-center gap-2 ${receptionTab === 'produtos' ? 'bg-[#C9A356] text-black shadow' : 'bg-[#181612] text-gray-300 hover:bg-[#221F18]'}`}
                      >
                        <Icons.Package className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                        <span>Produtos & Bar (PDV)</span>
                      </button>
                      {currentUser.role === 'dono' && (
                        <>
                          <button
                            onClick={() => setReceptionTab('financeiro')}
                            className={`px-5 py-2 rounded-full text-xs font-serif uppercase tracking-wider font-bold transition-all flex items-center gap-2 ${receptionTab === 'financeiro' ? 'bg-[#C9A356] text-black shadow' : 'bg-[#181612] text-gray-300 hover:bg-[#221F18]'}`}
                          >
                            <Icons.TrendingUp className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                            <span>DRE & Caixa</span>
                          </button>
                          <button
                            onClick={() => setReceptionTab('escalas')}
                            className={`px-5 py-2 rounded-full text-xs font-serif uppercase tracking-wider font-bold transition-all flex items-center gap-2 ${receptionTab === 'escalas' ? 'bg-[#C9A356] text-black shadow' : 'bg-[#181612] text-gray-300 hover:bg-[#221F18]'}`}
                          >
                            <Icons.Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                            <span>Escalas da Equipe</span>
                          </button>
                          <button
                            onClick={() => setReceptionTab('equipe')}
                            className={`px-5 py-2 rounded-full text-xs font-serif uppercase tracking-wider font-bold transition-all flex items-center gap-2 ${receptionTab === 'equipe' ? 'bg-[#C9A356] text-black shadow' : 'bg-[#181612] text-gray-300 hover:bg-[#221F18]'}`}
                          >
                            <Icons.Users className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                            <span>Quadro de Barbeiros</span>
                          </button>
                        </>
                      )}
                    </>
                  )}
                </div>
              </header>

              {/* CONTE├ÜDO */}
              <main className="max-w-7xl mx-auto p-6 flex-1 w-full space-y-6">
                
                {/* 1. SE├ç├âO DO BARBEIRO */}
                {currentUser.role === 'barbeiro' && (
                  <div className="space-y-6">
                    {barberTab === 'minha_agenda' && (
                      <div className="space-y-6">
                        <div className="bg-[#161410] p-6 rounded-3xl border border-[#282319] flex flex-col lg:flex-row justify-between items-start lg:items-center gap-4 shadow-xl">
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                            <img src={currentUser.avatar} alt={currentUser.name} className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full object-cover border-2 border-[#C9A356] shadow" />
                            <div>
                              <div className="text-[10px] font-serif uppercase tracking-widest text-[#C9A356] font-bold">AGENDA PESSOAL DO PROFISSIONAL</div>
                              <h2 className="font-serif text-2xl font-bold text-white mt-0.5">{currentUser.name}</h2>
                              <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">Escala: <strong>{barberEditStartTime} ├ás {barberEditEndTime}</strong> ÔÇó Almo├ºo: <strong>{barberEditLunchBreak}</strong></p>
                            </div>
                          </div>

                          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                            <div className="bg-[#1E1B15] p-1 rounded-full border border-[#332C20] flex items-center gap-1">
                              <button onClick={() => setBarberCalendarMode('semanal')} className={`px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full text-xs font-serif font-bold transition-all ${barberCalendarMode === 'semanal' ? 'bg-[#C9A356] text-black shadow' : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-white'}`}>
                                Semana
                              </button>
                              <button onClick={() => setBarberCalendarMode('mensal')} className={`px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full text-xs font-serif font-bold transition-all ${barberCalendarMode === 'mensal' ? 'bg-[#C9A356] text-black shadow' : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-white'}`}>
                                M├¬s
                              </button>
                              <button onClick={() => setBarberCalendarMode('comparativo')} className={`px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full text-xs font-serif font-bold transition-all ${barberCalendarMode === 'comparativo' ? 'bg-[#C9A356] text-black shadow' : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-white'}`}>
                                Grade Hor├írios
                              </button>
                              <button onClick={() => setBarberCalendarMode('diaria')} className={`px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full text-xs font-serif font-bold transition-all ${barberCalendarMode === 'diaria' ? 'bg-[#C9A356] text-black shadow' : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-white'}`}>
                                Hoje
                              </button>
                            </div>
                            <button
                              onClick={() => { setBookingForm(prev => ({ ...prev, barberKey: currentUser.barberKey })); setIsBookingModalOpen(true); }}
                              className="px-4 py-2 bg-[#C9A356] hover:bg-[#B59044] text-black font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase rounded-full shadow flex items-center gap-1.5"
                            >
                              <Icons.Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Encaixe
                            </button>
                          </div>
                        </div>

                        {(barberCalendarMode === 'semanal' || barberCalendarMode === 'comparativo') && (
                          <div className="bg-[#161410] p-4 rounded-2xl border border-[#282319] flex justify-between items-center shadow">
                            <button disabled={selectedWeekIndex === 0} onClick={() => setSelectedWeekIndex(Math.max(0, selectedWeekIndex - 1))} className={`px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full text-xs font-serif font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-all ${selectedWeekIndex === 0 ? 'opacity-30 cursor-not-allowed text-gray-500' : 'bg-[#1E1B15] text-[#C9A356] hover:bg-[#2A251D]'}`}>
                              <span>ÔÇ╣</span><span>Semana Anterior</span>
                            </button>
                            <div className="text-center">
                              <span className="text-[10px] font-serif uppercase tracking-widest text-[#C9A356] font-bold block">{currentWeek.sub}</span>
                              <strong className="font-serif text-sm text-white">{currentWeek.label}</strong>
                            </div>
                            <button disabled={selectedWeekIndex === WEEKS_DATA.length - 1} onClick={() => setSelectedWeekIndex(Math.min(WEEKS_DATA.length - 1, selectedWeekIndex + 1))} className={`px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full text-xs font-serif font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-all ${selectedWeekIndex === WEEKS_DATA.length - 1 ? 'opacity-30 cursor-not-allowed text-gray-500' : 'bg-[#1E1B15] text-[#C9A356] hover:bg-[#2A251D]'}`}>
                              <span>Pr├│xima Semana</span><span>ÔÇ║</span>
                            </button>
                          </div>
                        )}

                        {barberCalendarMode === 'semanal' && (
                          <div className="space-y-4">
                            <div className="grid grid-cols-6 gap-2 sm:gap-3">
                              {currentWeek.days.map((d) => {
                                const dayAppts = myAllAppointments.filter(a => (a.day === d.name && (a.weekId === currentWeek.id || (currentWeek.id === 0 && a.weekId === undefined))));
                                const isSelected = selectedWeekDay === d.name;
                                return (
                                  <button key={d.name} onClick={() => setSelectedWeekDay(d.name)} className={`p-3.5 rounded-2xl border text-center transition-all ${isSelected ? 'bg-[#C9A356] text-black border-[#C9A356] shadow-xl scale-[1.02]' : 'bg-[#161410] text-gray-300 border-[#282319] hover:border-[#C9A356]/50'}`}>
                                    <div className="text-[10px] font-serif uppercase font-bold">{d.name}</div>
                                    <div className="text-lg font-bold font-serif my-0.5">{d.num}</div>
                                    <div className={`text-[10px] rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 font-bold ${isSelected ? 'bg-black/30 text-black' : 'bg-[#221E17] text-[#C9A356]'}`}>
                                      {dayAppts.length} {dayAppts.length === 1 ? 'cliente' : 'clientes'}
                                    </div>
                                  </button>
                                );
                              })}
                            </div>

                            <div className="bg-[#161410] p-6 rounded-3xl border border-[#282319] shadow-xl">
                              <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6 pb-4 border-b border-[#282319]">
                                <div>
                                  <span className="text-[10px] font-serif uppercase tracking-widest text-[#C9A356] font-bold">HOR├üRIOS DE {selectedWeekDay.toUpperCase()} ({currentWeek.label})</span>
                                  <h3 className="font-serif text-lg font-bold text-white">Clientes & Agendamentos</h3>
                                </div>
                                <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">Jornada: <strong>{barberEditStartTime} ├ás {barberEditEndTime}</strong></div>
                              </div>

                              <div className="space-y-3">
                                {myAllAppointments.filter(a => (a.day === selectedWeekDay && (a.weekId === currentWeek.id || (currentWeek.id === 0 && a.weekId === undefined)))).map((a) => (
                                  <div key={a.id} className="bg-[#1E1B15] p-4 rounded-2xl border border-[#30291D] flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-start sm:items-center gap-3 hover:border-[#C9A356]/40 transition-colors">
                                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                                      <div className="w-16 text-center py-2 bg-[#12110E] rounded-xl border border-[#282319]">
                                        <div className="font-serif font-bold text-sm text-[#C9A356]">{a.time}</div>
                                        <div className="text-[9px] text-gray-400">{a.day}</div>
                                      </div>
                                      <div>
                                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                          <span className="font-serif font-bold text-sm text-white">{a.client}</span>
                                          <StatusBadge status={a.status} />
                                        </div>
                                        <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5">{a.service} ÔÇó R$ {a.price.toFixed(2).replace('.', ',')}</div>
                                        <div className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 italic mt-0.5">"{a.notes}"</div>
                                      </div>
                                    </div>

                                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 self-end sm:self-auto">
                                      {a.status === 'agendado' && (
                                        <button onClick={() => changeAppointmentStatus(a.id, 'aguardando')} className="px-3 py-1 bg-[#282319] hover:bg-[#C9A356] hover:text-black text-gray-300 rounded-full text-xs font-serif font-bold transition-all">
                                          Chegou
                                        </button>
                                      )}
                                      {a.status === 'aguardando' && (
                                        <button onClick={() => changeAppointmentStatus(a.id, 'em_atendimento')} className="px-3 py-1 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white rounded-full text-xs font-serif font-bold transition-all">
                                          Chamar Cadeira
                                        </button>
                                      )}
                                      {a.status === 'em_atendimento' && (
                                        <button onClick={() => openCheckout(a)} className="px-3 py-1 bg-[#C9A356] hover:bg-[#B59044] text-black rounded-full text-xs font-serif font-bold transition-all shadow">
                                          Fechar Comanda
                                        </button>
                                      )}

                                      <button onClick={() => openEditAppointment(a)} className="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-[#25221B] hover:bg-[#C9A356] hover:text-black text-gray-400 transition-all" title="Editar">
                                        <Icons.Edit className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                                      </button>
                                      <button onClick={() => deleteAppointment(a.id)} className="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-[#25221B] hover:bg-red-900 text-gray-400 hover:text-white transition-all" title="Excluir">
                                        <Icons.Trash className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                                      </button>
                                    </div>
                                  </div>
                                ))}

                                {myAllAppointments.filter(a => (a.day === selectedWeekDay && (a.weekId === currentWeek.id || (currentWeek.id === 0 && a.weekId === undefined)))).length === 0 && (
                                  <div className="text-center py-12 text-gray-500 text-xs font-serif">
                                    Nenhum agendamento para este dia nesta semana. Hor├írios livres!
                                  </div>
                                )}
                              </div>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        )}

                        {barberCalendarMode === 'mensal' && (
                          <div className="bg-[#161410] p-6 rounded-3xl border border-[#282319] shadow-xl space-y-5">
                            <div className="flex justify-between items-center pb-4 border-b border-[#282319]">
                              <div>
                                <span className="text-[10px] font-serif uppercase tracking-widest text-[#C9A356] font-bold">CALEND├üRIO MENSAL DO BARBEIRO</span>
                                <h3 className="font-serif text-xl font-bold text-white mt-0.5">Setembro 2026</h3>
                              </div>
                              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">Clique em um dia para inspecionar clientes</div>
                            </div>

                            <div className="grid grid-cols-7 gap-2 text-center text-[11px] font-serif font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase pb-2">
                              <span>Seg</span><span>Ter</span><span>Qua</span><span>Qui</span><span>Sex</span><span>S├íb</span><span className="text-red-400">Dom</span>
                            </div>

                            <div className="grid grid-cols-7 gap-2">
                              <div className="bg-transparent rounded-2xl p-2 opacity-0"></div>
                              {SEPTEMBER_DAYS.map((d) => {
                                const dayAppts = myAllAppointments.filter(a => a.dayNum === d.dayNum);
                                const isSelected = selectedMonthDay === d.dayNum;
                                return (
                                  <button key={d.dayNum} onClick={() => setSelectedMonthDay(d.dayNum)} className={`p-3 rounded-2xl border text-center transition-all flex flex-col justify-between aspect-square ${isSelected ? 'bg-[#C9A356] text-black border-[#C9A356] shadow-xl scale-105 font-bold' : d.isOpen ? 'bg-[#1C1A14] text-gray-200 border-[#2C261D] hover:border-[#C9A356]/50' : 'bg-[#12110E] text-gray-600 border-transparent cursor-not-allowed'}`}>
                                    <div className="flex justify-between items-center w-full">
                                      <span className="text-xs font-serif font-bold">{d.dayNum}</span>
                                      <span className="text-[9px] uppercase">{d.weekDay}</span>
                                    </div>
                                    <div className="mt-1">
                                      {d.isOpen ? (
                                        dayAppts.length > 0 ? (
                                          <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full ${isSelected ? 'bg-black text-[#C9A356]' : 'bg-[#C9A356]/20 text-[#C9A356]'}`}>
                                            {dayAppts.length} {dayAppts.length === 1 ? 'corte' : 'cortes'}
                                          </span>
                                        ) : (
                                          <span className="text-[9px] text-gray-500">Livre</span>
                                        )
                                      ) : (
                                        <span className="text-[9px] text-red-500">Folga</span>
                                      )}
                                    </div>
                                  </button>
                                );
                              })}
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        )}

                        {barberCalendarMode === 'comparativo' && (
                          <div className="bg-[#161410] p-6 rounded-3xl border border-[#282319] shadow-xl space-y-5">
                            <div>
                              <span className="text-[10px] font-serif uppercase tracking-widest text-[#C9A356] font-bold">MAPA DE CADEIRAS & HOR├üRIOS</span>
                              <h3 className="font-serif text-xl font-bold text-white mt-0.5">Ocupa├º├úo por Barbeiro em {selectedWeekDay} ({currentWeek.label})</h3>
                              <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">Consulte o status exato de cada profissional em cada slot de atendimento.</p>
                            </div>

                            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                              <table className="w-full text-left text-xs border-collapse">
                                <thead>
                                  <tr className="border-b border-[#2D271E] text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase text-[10px]">
                                    <th className="py-3 px-3">Hor├írio</th>
                                    <th className="py-3 px-3">Luis Valverde (Cadeira 1)</th>
                                    <th className="py-3 px-3">Yago Barber (Cadeira 2)</th>
                                  </tr>
                                </thead>
                                <tbody className="divide-y divide-[#221E17]">
                                  {TIME_SLOTS_COMPARE.map((slot) => {
                                    const luisAppt = appointments.find(a => a.barber === 'Luis Valverde' && a.day === selectedWeekDay && a.time === slot && (a.weekId === currentWeek.id || (currentWeek.id === 0 && a.weekId === undefined)));
                                    const yagoAppt = appointments.find(a => a.barber === 'Yago Barber' && a.day === selectedWeekDay && a.time === slot && (a.weekId === currentWeek.id || (currentWeek.id === 0 && a.weekId === undefined)));
                                    const isLuisLunch = slot === '12:45' || slot === '13:30';
                                    const isYagoLunch = slot === '13:30' || slot === '14:15';

                                    return (
                                      <tr key={slot} className="hover:bg-[#1B1914] transition-colors">
                                        <td className="py-3 px-3 font-serif font-bold text-[#C9A356] whitespace-nowrap">{slot}</td>
                                        <td className="py-3 px-3">
                                          {luisAppt ? (
                                            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-red-950/40 border border-red-800/40 text-red-200 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-medium">
                                              <span>ÔùÅ</span>
                                              <span>Ocupado: <strong>{luisAppt.client}</strong> ({luisAppt.service})</span>
                                            </span>
                                          ) : isLuisLunch ? (
                                            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-[#252016] text-amber-400 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px]">
                                              <span>Ôÿò</span>
                                              <span>Intervalo de Almo├ºo</span>
                                            </span>
                                          ) : (
                                            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-green-950/40 border border-green-800/40 text-green-300 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px]">
                                              <span>Ô£ô</span>
                                              <span>Hor├írio Livre (Cadeira 1)</span>
                                            </span>
                                          )}
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="py-3 px-3">
                                          {selectedWeekDay === 'Seg' ? (
                                            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-[#1F1C16] text-gray-500 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px]">
                                              <span>Folga Semanal (Inicia Ter├ºa)</span>
                                            </span>
                                          ) : yagoAppt ? (
                                            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-red-950/40 border border-red-800/40 text-red-200 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-medium">
                                              <span>ÔùÅ</span>
                                              <span>Ocupado: <strong>{yagoAppt.client}</strong> ({yagoAppt.service})</span>
                                            </span>
                                          ) : isYagoLunch ? (
                                            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-[#252016] text-amber-400 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px]">
                                              <span>Ôÿò</span>
                                              <span>Intervalo de Almo├ºo</span>
                                            </span>
                                          ) : (
                                            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-green-950/40 border border-green-800/40 text-green-300 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px]">
                                              <span>Ô£ô</span>
                                              <span>Hor├írio Livre (Cadeira 2)</span>
                                            </span>
                                          )}
                                        </td>
                                      </tr>
                                    );
                                  })}
                                </tbody>
                              </table>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        )}

                        {barberCalendarMode === 'diaria' && (
                          <div className="bg-[#161410] p-6 rounded-3xl border border-[#282319] shadow-xl">
                            <h3 className="font-serif text-lg font-bold text-white mb-4">Meus Atendimentos de Hoje (Segunda-feira)</h3>
                            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                              {myTodaysAppointments.map((a) => (
                                <div key={a.id} className="bg-[#1A1813] p-5 rounded-3xl border border-[#2D271E] flex flex-col justify-between">
                                  <div>
                                    <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
                                      <div className="font-serif font-bold text-base text-white">{a.client}</div>
                                      <span className="font-serif font-bold text-sm text-[#C9A356] bg-[#231F17] px-3 py-1 rounded-full">
                                        {a.time}
                                      </span>
                                    </div>
                                    <div className="text-xs text-gray-300 font-medium mb-1">{a.service}</div>
                                    <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 mb-3">{a.phone} ÔÇó {a.notes}</div>
                                  </div>

                                  <div className="pt-3 border-t border-[#2A2419] flex justify-between items-center">
                                    <div>
                                      <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-400">Minha Comiss├úo: </span>
                                      <strong className="text-green-400 text-xs font-serif">R$ {(a.price * myCommissionRate).toFixed(2).replace('.', ',')}</strong>
                                    </div>
                                    <div className="flex gap-2">
                                      <button onClick={() => openEditAppointment(a)} className="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-[#25221B] hover:bg-[#C9A356] hover:text-black text-gray-400 transition-all">
                                        <Icons.Edit className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                                      </button>
                                      {a.status === 'aguardando' && (
                                        <button onClick={() => changeAppointmentStatus(a.id, 'em_atendimento')} className="px-3 py-1 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold uppercase">
                                          Chamar
                                        </button>
                                      )}
                                      {a.status === 'em_atendimento' && (
                                        <button onClick={() => openCheckout(a)} className="px-3 py-1 bg-[#C9A356] hover:bg-[#B59044] text-black rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold uppercase">
                                          Concluir
                                        </button>
                                      )}
                                    </div>
                                  </div>
                                </div>
                              ))}
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        )}

                      </div>
                    )}

                    {barberTab === 'meus_ganhos' && (
                      <div className="space-y-6">
                        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                          <div className="bg-[#161410] p-5 rounded-3xl border border-[#282319] shadow">
                            <div className="text-[10px] font-serif uppercase tracking-widest text-[#C9A356] font-bold">MINHA COMISS├âO HOJE</div>
                            <div className="font-serif text-2xl font-bold text-green-400 mt-1">R$ {myCommissionToday.toFixed(2).replace('.', ',')}</div>
                            <div className="text-[11px] text-gray-400 mt-1">Taxa: {(myCommissionRate * 100)}% sobre servi├ºos</div>
                          </div>
                          <div className="bg-[#161410] p-5 rounded-3xl border border-[#282319] shadow">
                            <div className="text-[10px] font-serif uppercase tracking-widest text-[#C9A356] font-bold">CLIENTES ATENDIDOS</div>

                            <div className="text-[11px] text-gray-400 mt-1">Atendimentos conclu├¡dos hoje</div>
                          </div>
                          <div className="bg-[#161410] p-5 rounded-3xl border border-[#282319] shadow">
                            <div className="text-[10px] font-serif uppercase tracking-widest text-[#C9A356] font-bold">PREVIS├âO DA SEMANA</div>
                            <div className="font-serif text-2xl font-bold text-[#C9A356] mt-1">
                              R$ {(myWeekAppointments.reduce((acc, a) => acc + a.price, 0) * myCommissionRate).toFixed(2).replace('.', ',')}
                            </div>
                            <div className="text-[11px] text-gray-400 mt-1">{myWeekAppointments.length} cortes nesta semana</div>
                          </div>
                        </div>

                        <div className="bg-[#161410] p-6 rounded-3xl border border-[#282319] shadow-xl">
                          <h3 className="font-serif text-lg font-bold text-white mb-4">Extrato Pessoal de Servi├ºos</h3>
                          <div className="space-y-3">
                            {myWeekAppointments.map((a) => (
                              <div key={a.id} className="p-3.5 bg-[#1B1914] rounded-2xl border border-[#2C271E] flex justify-between items-center text-xs">
                                <div>
                                  <div className="font-serif font-bold text-white">{a.client} ÔÇö {a.service}</div>
                                  <div className="text-gray-400 text-[11px]">{a.day} ÔÇó {a.time} ÔÇó Valor Bruto: R$ {a.price.toFixed(2).replace('.', ',')}</div>

                                  <div className="font-serif font-bold text-green-400 text-sm">+ R$ {(a.price * myCommissionRate).toFixed(2).replace('.', ',')}</div>
                                  <StatusBadge status={a.status} />
                                </div>
                              </div>
                            ))}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )}

                    {barberTab === 'minha_escala' && (
                      <div className="bg-[#161410] p-6 sm:p-8 rounded-3xl border border-[#282319] shadow-xl space-y-6">
                        <div>
                          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 bg-[#C9A356]/15 border border-[#C9A356]/40 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-serif font-bold text-[#C9A356] mb-2">
                            <Icons.Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                            <span>AUTONOMIA DO PROFISSIONAL</span>

                            Voc├¬ pode ajustar seus hor├írios de in├¡cio e t├®rmino, seu intervalo de almo├ºo e quais dias da semana atende. Suas mudan├ºas passam a valer imediatamente na agenda p├║blica e recep├º├úo!
                          </p>
                        </div>

                        {scheduleSavedToast && (
                          <div className="p-4 bg-green-950/80 border border-green-500 rounded-2xl text-green-200 text-xs font-serif flex items-center gap-3 shadow-xl">
                            <Icons.Check className="w-5 h-5 text-green-400" />
                            <div>
                              <strong className="block text-white">Hor├írios Salvos com Sucesso!</strong>

                            </div>
                          </div>

                        <form onSubmit={handleSaveBarberSchedule} className="space-y-6 max-w-2xl bg-[#1B1914] p-6 rounded-2xl border border-[#2E281F]">
                          <div>
                            <label className="block text-xs font-serif uppercase tracking-wider text-[#C9A356] font-bold mb-2">
                              Dias que Voc├¬ Atende na Barbearia:

                              {['Seg', 'Ter', 'Qua', 'Qui', 'Sex', 'S├íb'].map(day => {
                                const isActive = barberEditActiveDays.includes(day);
                                return (
                                  <button key={day} type="button" onClick={() => toggleBarberDay(day)} className={`py-2 px-3 rounded-xl text-xs font-serif font-bold transition-all text-center ${isActive ? 'bg-[#C9A356] text-black shadow' : 'bg-[#12110E] text-gray-400 border border-[#282319]'}`}>
                                    {day}
                                  </button>
                                );
                              })}
                            </div>
                          </div>

                          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                            <div>
                              <label className="block text-xs font-serif uppercase tracking-wider text-gray-300 mb-1">Hor├írio de In├¡cio:</label>
                              <select value={barberEditStartTime} onChange={(e) => setBarberEditStartTime(e.target.value)} className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-xs text-white">
                                <option value="08:30">08:30</option>
                                <option value="09:00">09:00</option>
                                <option value="09:30">09:30</option>
                                <option value="10:00">10:00 (Padr├úo Fade)</option>
                                <option value="10:30">10:30</option>
                              </select>
                            </div>
                            <div>
                              <label className="block text-xs font-serif uppercase tracking-wider text-gray-300 mb-1">Hor├írio de T├®rmino:</label>
                              <select value={barberEditEndTime} onChange={(e) => setBarberEditEndTime(e.target.value)} className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-xs text-white">
                                <option value="17:00">17:00</option>
                                <option value="18:00">18:00</option>
                                <option value="18:30">18:30</option>
                                <option value="19:00">19:00 (Padr├úo Sal├úo)</option>
                                <option value="20:00">20:00</option>
                              </select>
                            </div>
                          </div>

                          <div>
                            <label className="block text-xs font-serif uppercase tracking-wider text-gray-300 mb-1">Intervalo de Almo├ºo / Pausa:</label>
                            <select value={barberEditLunchBreak} onChange={(e) => setBarberEditLunchBreak(e.target.value)} className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-xs text-white">
                              <option value="12:00 ├ás 13:00">12:00 ├ás 13:00</option>
                              <option value="12:30 ├ás 13:30">12:30 ├ás 13:30 (Padr├úo Mestre)</option>
                              <option value="13:00 ├ás 14:00">13:00 ├ás 14:00 (Padr├úo Yago)</option>
                              <option value="13:30 ├ás 14:30">13:30 ├ás 14:30</option>

                          <div className="pt-3 border-t border-[#2A261F] flex justify-end">
                            <button type="submit" className="px-6 py-3 bg-[#C9A356] hover:bg-[#B59044] text-black font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider rounded-xl transition-all shadow-xl flex items-center gap-2">
                              <Icons.Save className="w-4 h-4" />
                              <span>Salvar Minha Escala</span>
                            </button>
                          </div>
                        </form>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* 2. SE├ç├âO DO DONO E RECEP├ç├âO */}
                {(currentUser.role === 'dono' || currentUser.role === 'recepcao') && (
                  <div className="space-y-6">
                    {receptionTab === 'agenda' && (
                      <div className="space-y-6">
                        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-start sm:items-center bg-[#161410] p-6 rounded-3xl border border-[#282319] gap-4 shadow-xl">
                          <div>
                            <div className="text-[10px] font-serif uppercase tracking-widest text-[#C9A356] font-bold">VIS├âO GERAL DO SAL├âO</div>
                            <h2 className="font-serif text-2xl font-bold text-white mt-0.5">Agenda das Cadeiras</h2>
                            <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">Segunda-feira (08/09) ÔÇó Acompanhamento e edi├º├úo direta de atendimentos.</p>
                          </div>
                          <div className="flex gap-3">
                            <button onClick={() => setIsQuickSaleModalOpen(true)} className="px-4 py-2 bg-[#1C1A14] hover:bg-[#25221B] text-[#C9A356] border border-[#C9A356]/40 font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase rounded-full transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5">
                              <Icons.Package className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                              <span>Venda Balc├úo (PDV)</span>
                            </button>
                            <button
                              onClick={() => { setBookingForm(prev => ({ ...prev, barberKey: 'luis' })); setIsBookingModalOpen(true); }}
                              className="px-5 py-2 bg-[#C9A356] hover:bg-[#B59044] text-black font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase rounded-full shadow flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-all"
                            >
                              <Icons.Plus className="w-4 h-4" /> Novo Encaixe
                            </button>
                          </div>
                        </div>

                        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
                          {barbers.map((barber) => {
                            const barberAppts = todaysAppointments.filter(a => a.barber === barber.name);
                            const sched = barberSchedules.find(b => b.id === barber.barberKey) || barber;
                            return (
                              <div key={barber.id} className="bg-[#161410] p-6 rounded-3xl border border-[#282319] shadow-xl">

                                  </div>
                                  <span className="text-xs text-gray-400">{barberAppts.length} clientes hoje</span>
                                </div>

                                <div className="space-y-3">
                                  {barberAppts.map((a) => (
                                    <div key={a.id} className="p-3.5 bg-[#1B1914] rounded-2xl border border-[#2C271E] flex justify-between items-center text-xs hover:border-[#C9A356]/40 transition-colors">
                                      <div>
                                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                          <span className="font-serif font-bold text-sm text-[#C9A356]">{a.time}</span>
                                          <strong className="text-white text-xs">{a.client}</strong>
                                          <StatusBadge status={a.status} />
                                        </div>
                                        <div className="text-gray-400 text-[11px] mt-0.5">{a.service} ÔÇö R$ {a.price.toFixed(2).replace('.', ',')}</div>
                                      </div>
                                      
                                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                        {a.status !== 'concluido' && (
                                          <button onClick={() => openCheckout(a)} className="px-3 py-1 bg-[#C9A356] hover:bg-[#B59044] text-black rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold uppercase transition-all shadow">
                                            Cobrar
                                          </button>
                                        )}
                                        <button onClick={() => openEditAppointment(a)} className="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-[#24211A] hover:bg-[#C9A356] hover:text-black text-gray-400 transition-all" title="Editar">
                                          <Icons.Edit className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                                        </button>
                                        <button onClick={() => deleteAppointment(a.id)} className="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-[#24211A] hover:bg-red-900 text-gray-400 hover:text-white transition-all" title="Excluir">
                                          <Icons.Trash className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                                        </button>
                                      </div>
                                    </div>

                                      Nenhum cliente agendado nesta cadeira hoje.
                                    </div>
                                  )}
                                </div>
                              </div>
                            );
                          })}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )}

                    {receptionTab === 'fila' && (
                      <div className="space-y-6">
                        <div className="bg-[#161410] p-6 rounded-3xl border border-[#282319] shadow-xl">
                          <h3 className="font-serif text-xl font-bold text-white">Controle do Sof├í & Lounge</h3>
                          <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1">Acompanhe quem est├í no caf├® esperando corte e quem est├í na cadeira.</p>

                        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
                          <div className="bg-[#15130F] p-5 rounded-3xl border border-[#262118]">
                            <div className="font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase text-gray-300 pb-3 mb-3 border-b border-[#282319] flex justify-between items-center">
                              <span>1. Agendados</span>
                              <span className="text-[#C9A356]">{scheduledToday.length}</span>
                            </div>
                            <div className="space-y-3">
                              {scheduledToday.map(a => (
                                <div key={a.id} className="p-3 bg-[#1C1914] rounded-2xl border border-[#282319] text-xs space-y-2">
                                  <div className="flex justify-between font-bold text-white">
                                    <span>{a.client}</span>
                                    <span className="text-[#C9A356] font-serif">{a.time}</span>
                                  </div>
                                  <div className="text-gray-400 text-[11px]">{a.service} com {a.barber}</div>
                                  <button onClick={() => changeAppointmentStatus(a.id, 'aguardando')} className="w-full py-1.5 bg-[#25221B] hover:bg-[#C9A356] hover:text-black text-white rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold uppercase transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5">
                                    <Icons.Coffee className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                                    <span>Marcar Chegada</span>
                                  </button>
                                </div>

                            </div>
                          </div>

                          <div className="bg-[#17140E] p-5 rounded-3xl border border-amber-500/40">
                            <div className="font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase text-amber-300 pb-3 mb-3 border-b border-amber-500/30 flex justify-between items-center">
                              <span>2. No Lounge / Caf├®</span>
                              <span className="text-amber-400">{waitingToday.length}</span>

                              {waitingToday.map(a => (

                                  <div className="font-bold text-white">{a.client}</div>
                                  <div className="text-gray-300 text-[11px]">{a.service} com {a.barber}</div>
                                  <button onClick={() => changeAppointmentStatus(a.id, 'em_atendimento')} className="w-full py-1.5 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold uppercase transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5">
                                    <Icons.Scissors className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                                    <span>Chamar p/ Cadeira</span>
                                  </button>
                                </div>
                              ))}
                            </div>
                          </div>

                          <div className="bg-[#151411] p-5 rounded-3xl border border-blue-500/40">
                            <div className="font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase text-blue-300 pb-3 mb-3 border-b border-blue-500/30 flex justify-between items-center">
                              <span>3. Na Cadeira</span>
                              <span className="text-blue-400">{inChairToday.length}</span>
                            </div>
                            <div className="space-y-3">
                              {inChairToday.map(a => (
                                <div key={a.id} className="p-3 bg-[#1C1A14] rounded-2xl border border-blue-500/30 text-xs space-y-2">
                                  <div className="font-bold text-white">{a.client}</div>
                                  <div className="text-gray-300 text-[11px]">{a.service} com {a.barber}</div>
                                  <button onClick={() => openCheckout(a)} className="w-full py-1.5 bg-[#C9A356] hover:bg-[#B59044] text-black rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold uppercase transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 shadow">
                                    <Icons.CreditCard className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />

                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )}

                    {receptionTab === 'produtos' && (
                      <div className="space-y-6">
                        <div className="bg-[#161410] p-6 rounded-3xl border border-[#282319] shadow-xl flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-start sm:items-center gap-4">
                          <div>
                            <h3 className="font-serif text-xl font-bold text-white">Estoque do Bar & Cosm├®ticos</h3>

                          </div>
                          <div className="flex gap-2">
                            <button
                              onClick={() => setIsNewProductModalOpen(true)}
                              className="px-4 py-2 bg-[#1C1A14] hover:bg-[#C9A356] text-[#C9A356] hover:text-black border border-[#C9A356]/40 font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase rounded-full transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5"
                            >
                              <Icons.Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Novo Produto
                            </button>
                            <button
                              onClick={() => setIsQuickSaleModalOpen(true)}
                              className="px-5 py-2 bg-[#C9A356] hover:bg-[#B59044] text-black font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase rounded-full shadow transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5"
                            >
                              <Icons.Package className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Venda Balc├úo
                            </button>
                          </div>
                        </div>

                        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                          {products.map(p => (
                            <div key={p.id} className="bg-[#171511] p-4 rounded-2xl border border-[#29241C] flex justify-between items-center text-xs">

                                <div className="font-bold text-white text-xs">{p.name}</div>
                                <div className="text-[11px] text-[#C9A356] font-serif">R$ {p.price.toFixed(2).replace('.', ',')}</div>

                              </div>
                              <div className="flex gap-1">
                                <button onClick={() => updateStock(p.id, -1)} className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-[#25211A] text-white hover:bg-red-900 font-bold transition-all">-</button>
                                <button onClick={() => updateStock(p.id, 1)} className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-[#25211A] text-white hover:bg-green-900 font-bold transition-all">+</button>
                              </div>
                            </div>
                          ))}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )}

                    {receptionTab === 'financeiro' && currentUser.role === 'dono' && (
                      <div className="space-y-6">
                        <div className="bg-[#161410] p-6 rounded-3xl border border-[#282319] shadow-xl">
                          <h3 className="font-serif text-xl font-bold text-white">Demonstrativo Financeiro & DRE da Valverd's</h3>
                          <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1">Vis├úo restrita e confidencial acess├¡vel exclusivamente pelo propriet├írio.</p>
                        </div>
