          {isBookingModalOpen && (
            <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-md">
              <div className="bg-[#161410] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-3xl max-w-lg w-full p-6 sm:p-8 text-white shadow-2xl space-y-5">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-start pb-4 border-b border-[#2A261F]">
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-serif uppercase tracking-widest text-[#C9A356] font-bold">AGENDAMENTO & ENCAIXE</span>
                    <h3 className="font-serif text-2xl font-bold text-white mt-1">Marcar Hor├írio</h3>
                  </div>
                  <button onClick={() => setIsBookingModalOpen(false)} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-white text-xl">Ô£ò</button>
                </div>

                <form onSubmit={handleCreateBooking} className="space-y-4 text-xs">
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Nome do Cliente:</label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        required
                        value={bookingForm.client}
                        onChange={e => setBookingForm({ ...bookingForm, client: e.target.value })}
                        placeholder="Ex: Carlos Eduardo"
                        className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-white text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-[#C9A356]"
                      />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">WhatsApp:</label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        required
                        value={bookingForm.phone}
                        onChange={e => setBookingForm({ ...bookingForm, phone: e.target.value })}
                        placeholder="(21) 98888-7777"
                        className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-white text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-[#C9A356]"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Servi├ºo:</label>
                    <select
                      value={bookingForm.serviceId}
                      onChange={e => setBookingForm({ ...bookingForm, serviceId: e.target.value })}
                      className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-white text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-[#C9A356]"
                    >
                      {services.map(s => (
                        <option key={s.id} value={s.id}>{s.name} ÔÇö R$ {s.price.toFixed(2).replace('.', ',')}</option>
                      ))}
                    </select>
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Barbeiro:</label>
                      <select
                        value={bookingForm.barberKey}
                        onChange={e => setBookingForm({ ...bookingForm, barberKey: e.target.value })}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-white text-xs focus:outline-none"
                      >
                        {barbers.map(b => (
                          <option key={b.id} value={b.barberKey}>{b.name.split(' ')[0]}</option>
                        ))}
                      </select>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Dia:</label>
                      <select
                        value={bookingForm.day}
                        onChange={e => setBookingForm({ ...bookingForm, day: e.target.value })}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-white text-xs focus:outline-none"
                      >
                        <option value="Seg">Segunda (08/09)</option>
                        <option value="Ter">Ter├ºa (09/09)</option>
                        <option value="Qua">Quarta (10/09)</option>
                        <option value="Qui">Quinta (11/09)</option>
                        <option value="Sex">Sexta (12/09)</option>
                        <option value="S├íb">S├íbado (13/09)</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Hor├írio:</label>
                      <select
                        value={bookingForm.time}
                        onChange={e => setBookingForm({ ...bookingForm, time: e.target.value })}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-white text-xs focus:outline-none"
                      >
                        {TIME_SLOTS_COMPARE.map(t => (
                          <option key={t} value={t}>{t}</option>
                        ))}
                      </select>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Observa├º├Áes:</label>
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={bookingForm.notes}
                      onChange={e => setBookingForm({ ...bookingForm, notes: e.target.value })}
                      placeholder="Ex: Degrad├¬ cl├íssico, toalha quente..."
                      className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-white text-xs focus:outline-none"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div className="pt-3 flex gap-3">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setIsBookingModalOpen(false)}
                      className="flex-1 py-3 bg-[#1F1C16] hover:bg-[#2A261F] text-gray-300 font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase rounded-xl transition-all"
                    >
                      Cancelar
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="submit"
                      className="flex-1 py-3 bg-[#C9A356] hover:bg-[#B59044] text-black font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider rounded-xl transition-all shadow-xl flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
                    >
                      <Icons.Check className="w-4 h-4" />
                      <span>Confirmar Hor├írio</span>
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </form>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* ========================================================================= */}
          {/* MODAL: EDITAR AGENDAMENTO                                                 */}
          {/* ========================================================================= */}
          {isEditApptModalOpen && editingAppt && (
            <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-md">
              <div className="bg-[#161410] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-3xl max-w-md w-full p-6 text-white shadow-2xl space-y-4">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-start pb-3 border-b border-[#2A261F]">
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-serif uppercase tracking-widest text-[#C9A356] font-bold">GERENCIAMENTO</span>
                    <h3 className="font-serif text-xl font-bold text-white mt-0.5">Editar Agendamento</h3>
                  </div>
                  <button onClick={() => setIsEditApptModalOpen(false)} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-white text-xl font-bold">Ô£ò</button>
                </div>

                <form onSubmit={handleSaveEditedAppt} className="space-y-4 text-xs">
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Nome do Cliente:</label>
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={editingAppt.client}
                      onChange={e => setEditingAppt({ ...editingAppt, client: e.target.value })}
                      className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-white"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Hor├írio:</label>
                      <select
                        value={editingAppt.time}
                        onChange={e => setEditingAppt({ ...editingAppt, time: e.target.value })}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-xs text-white"
                      >
                        {TIME_SLOTS_COMPARE.map(t => <option key={t} value={t}>{t}</option>)}
                      </select>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Status:</label>
                      <select
                        value={editingAppt.status}
                        onChange={e => setEditingAppt({ ...editingAppt, status: e.target.value })}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-white"
                      >
                        <option value="agendado">Agendado</option>
                        <option value="aguardando">No Lounge</option>
                        <option value="em_atendimento">Na Cadeira</option>
                        <option value="concluido">Conclu├¡do</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Observa├º├Áes:</label>
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={editingAppt.notes}
                      onChange={e => setEditingAppt({ ...editingAppt, notes: e.target.value })}
                      className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-xs text-white"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div className="pt-2 flex gap-3">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setIsEditApptModalOpen(false)}
                      className="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-[#1F1C16] text-gray-300 font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase rounded-xl"
                    >
                      Cancelar
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="submit"
                      className="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-[#C9A356] hover:bg-[#B59044] text-black font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase rounded-xl transition-all shadow flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
                    >
                      <Icons.Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      <span>Salvar Altera├º├Áes</span>
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </form>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* ========================================================================= */}
          {/* MODAL: FECHAMENTO DE COMANDA / CHECKOUT                                   */}
          {/* ========================================================================= */}
          {isCheckoutModalOpen && checkoutAppt && (
            <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-md">
              <div className="bg-[#161410] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-3xl max-w-lg w-full p-6 sm:p-8 text-white shadow-2xl space-y-5">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-start pb-4 border-b border-[#2A261F]">
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-serif uppercase tracking-widest text-[#C9A356] font-bold">FECHAMENTO DE COMANDA</span>
                    <h3 className="font-serif text-2xl font-bold text-white mt-1">{checkoutAppt.client}</h3>
                    <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">{checkoutAppt.service} com {checkoutAppt.barber}</p>
                  </div>
                  <button onClick={() => setIsCheckoutModalOpen(false)} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-white text-xl">Ô£ò</button>
                </div>

                <div className="space-y-4 text-xs">
                  <div className="bg-[#1C1A14] p-4 rounded-2xl border border-[#2D271E] space-y-2">
                    <span className="font-bold text-[#C9A356] uppercase tracking-wider block text-[10px]">ITENS CONSUMIDOS:</span>
                    {checkoutAppt.items.map((item, idx) => (
                      <div key={idx} className="flex justify-between py-1 border-b border-[#262118] last:border-none">
                        <span className="text-gray-200">{item.name}</span>
                        <strong className="font-serif text-[#C9A356]">R$ {item.price.toFixed(2).replace('.', ',')}</strong>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1 font-medium">+ Adicionar Bebida ou Pomada:</label>
                    <div className="flex gap-2">
                      <select
                        value={additionalItemId}
                        onChange={e => setAdditionalItemId(e.target.value)}
                        className="flex-1 px-3 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-xs text-white"
                      >
                        <option value="">Selecione um item do bar ou produto...</option>
                        {products.filter(p => p.stock > 0).map(p => (
                          <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name} ÔÇö R$ {p.price.toFixed(2).replace('.', ',')} (Estoque: {p.stock})</option>
                        ))}
                      </select>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => {
                          const prod = products.find(p => p.id === additionalItemId);
                          addItemToCheckout(prod);
                        }}
                        className="px-4 py-2 bg-[#25221B] hover:bg-[#C9A356] hover:text-black text-[#C9A356] font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase rounded-xl transition-all"
                      >
                        Adicionar
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="p-4 bg-[#1C1A14] rounded-2xl border border-green-500/30 flex justify-between items-center">
                    <span className="font-bold text-white text-sm uppercase">Total da Comanda:</span>
                    <strong className="font-serif text-3xl font-bold text-green-400">
                      R$ {checkoutAppt.total.toFixed(2).replace('.', ',')}
                    </strong>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1 font-medium">Forma de Pagamento:</label>
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                      {['PIX', 'CART├âO', 'DINHEIRO'].map(m => (
                        <button
                          key={m}
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => setSelectedPayment(m)}
                          className={`py-2 rounded-xl font-bold text-xs font-serif transition-all ${selectedPayment === m ? 'bg-[#C9A356] text-black shadow' : 'bg-[#1F1C16] text-gray-300'}`}
                        >
                          {m}
                        </button>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="pt-2 flex gap-3">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setIsCheckoutModalOpen(false)}
                      className="flex-1 py-3 bg-[#1F1C16] text-gray-300 font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase rounded-xl"
                    >
                      Cancelar
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={handleConfirmCheckout}
                      className="flex-1 py-3 bg-[#C9A356] hover:bg-[#B59044] text-black font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider rounded-xl shadow-xl flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
                    >
                      <Icons.Check className="w-4 h-4" />
                      <span>Confirmar Pagamento</span>
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* ========================================================================= */}
          {/* MODAL: VENDA R├üPIDA DE BALC├âO                                             */}
          {/* ========================================================================= */}
          {isQuickSaleModalOpen && (
            <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-md">
              <div className="bg-[#161410] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-3xl max-w-md w-full p-6 text-white shadow-2xl space-y-4">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-start pb-3 border-b border-[#2A261F]">
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-serif uppercase tracking-widest text-[#C9A356] font-bold">PDV BALC├âO</span>
                    <h3 className="font-serif text-xl font-bold text-white mt-1">Venda Avulsa de Produto</h3>
                  </div>
                  <button onClick={() => setIsQuickSaleModalOpen(false)} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-white text-xl font-bold">Ô£ò</button>
                </div>

                <form onSubmit={handleQuickSale} className="space-y-4 text-xs">
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Produto / Bebida:</label>
                    <select
                      value={quickSaleProduct ? quickSaleProduct.id : ''}
                      onChange={e => setQuickSaleProduct(products.find(p => p.id === e.target.value))}
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-xs text-white"
                    >
                      {products.map(p => (
                        <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name} ÔÇö R$ {p.price.toFixed(2).replace('.', ',')} ({p.stock} un)</option>
                      ))}
                    </select>
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Quantidade:</label>
                      <input
                        type="number"
                        min="1"
                        max={quickSaleProduct ? quickSaleProduct.stock : 10}
                        value={quickSaleQty}
                        onChange={e => setQuickSaleQty(parseInt(e.target.value) || 1)}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-xs text-white font-bold"
                      />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Pagamento:</label>
                      <select
                        value={quickSalePayment}
                        onChange={e => setQuickSalePayment(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-xs text-white"
                      >
                        <option value="PIX">PIX</option>
                        <option value="CART├âO">CART├âO</option>
                        <option value="DINHEIRO">DINHEIRO</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="p-4 bg-[#1C1A14] rounded-2xl border border-[#2A261F] flex justify-between items-center">
                    <span className="font-bold text-white uppercase">Total da Venda:</span>
                    <strong className="font-serif text-2xl text-green-400">
                      R$ {((quickSaleProduct ? quickSaleProduct.price : 0) * quickSaleQty).toFixed(2).replace('.', ',')}
                    </strong>
                  </div>

                  <div className="pt-2 flex gap-3">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setIsQuickSaleModalOpen(false)}
                      className="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-[#1F1C16] text-gray-300 font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase rounded-xl"
                    >
                      Cancelar
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="submit"
                      className="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-[#C9A356] hover:bg-[#B59044] text-black font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase rounded-xl transition-all shadow flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
                    >
                      <Icons.Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      <span>Finalizar Venda</span>
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </form>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* ========================================================================= */}
          {/* MODAL: NOVO PRODUTO NO ESTOQUE                                            */}
          {/* ========================================================================= */}
          {isNewProductModalOpen && (
            <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-md">
              <div className="bg-[#161410] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-3xl max-w-md w-full p-6 text-white shadow-2xl space-y-4">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-start pb-3 border-b border-[#2A261F]">
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-serif uppercase tracking-widest text-[#C9A356] font-bold">ESTOQUE & INVENT├üRIO</span>
                    <h3 className="font-serif text-xl font-bold text-white mt-1">Cadastrar Novo Produto</h3>
                  </div>
                  <button onClick={() => setIsNewProductModalOpen(false)} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-white text-xl font-bold">Ô£ò</button>
                </div>

                <form onSubmit={handleAddNewProduct} className="space-y-4 text-xs">
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Nome do Item:</label>
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      required
                      value={newProductForm.name}
                      onChange={e => setNewProductForm({ ...newProductForm, name: e.target.value })}
                      placeholder="Ex: Cerveja Stella Artois (330ml)"
                      className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-xs text-white"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Categoria:</label>
                      <select
                        value={newProductForm.category}
                        onChange={e => setNewProductForm({ ...newProductForm, category: e.target.value })}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-xs text-white"
                      >
                        <option value="Bar">Bar</option>
                        <option value="Produtos">Produtos</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Pre├ºo de Venda (R$):</label>
                      <input
                        type="number"
                        step="0.50"
                        required
                        value={newProductForm.price}
                        onChange={e => setNewProductForm({ ...newProductForm, price: e.target.value })}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-xs text-white font-bold"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Estoque Inicial:</label>
                      <input
                        type="number"
                        min="1"
                        value={newProductForm.stock}
                        onChange={e => setNewProductForm({ ...newProductForm, stock: e.target.value })}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-xs text-white font-bold"
                      />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Estoque M├¡nimo:</label>
                      <input
                        type="number"
                        min="1"
                        value={newProductForm.minStock}
                        onChange={e => setNewProductForm({ ...newProductForm, minStock: e.target.value })}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-xs text-white font-bold"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="pt-2 flex gap-3">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setIsNewProductModalOpen(false)}
                      className="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-[#1F1C16] text-gray-300 font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase rounded-xl"
                    >
                      Cancelar
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="submit"
                      className="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-[#C9A356] hover:bg-[#B59044] text-black font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase rounded-xl transition-all shadow flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
                    >
                      <Icons.Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      <span>Salvar no Estoque</span>
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </form>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

        </div>
      );
    }

    const root = ReactDOM.createRoot(document.getElementById('root'));
    root.render(<ValverdsApp />);
  </script>

</body></html>
